# K 開頭詞條

> 本檔收錄 1,307 條含中文對譯的詞目。

<a id="entry-k-000001"></a>
## k

*n.* 1. **開爾文** — the basic unit of thermodynamic temperature adopted under the Systeme International d'Unites
2. **鉀** — a light soft silver-white metallic element of the alkali metal group; oxidizes rapidly in air and reacts violently with water; is abundant in nature in combined forms occurring in sea water and in carnallite and kainite and sylvite
3. **千** — the cardinal number that is the product of 10 and 100
4. **千字節** — a unit of information equal to 1000 bytes
5. **千字節** — a unit of information equal to 1024 bytes
6. **克他命** — street names for ketamine
*adj.* 1. **一千, 一千+的, 千** — denoting a quantity consisting of 1,000 items or units

<a id="entry-k-000002"></a>
## K-drama

*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **韓國電視劇 /韓國電視劇, 韓劇 /韓劇** — Dramatic television series produced in South Korea.

## k-dur 20

*n.* 1. **氯化鉀** — salt of potassium (KCl) (trade names K-Dur 20, Kaochlor and K-lor and Klorvess and K-lyte); taken in tablet form to treat potassium deficiency

*“he was keenly aware of his own shortcomings”*

*“he argued with great acuteness”*

2. a positive feeling of wanting to push ahead with something
3. thinness of edge or fineness of point

<a id="entry-k-000294"></a>
## keep

**發音：** /kiːp/
*v.* 1. **使保持某狀態, 維持, 保持** — keep in a certain state, position, or activity; e.g., "keep clean"
*“hold in place”*
*“She always held herself as a lady”*
2. **進行下去, 延續, 持續, 繼續, 保持** — continue a certain state, condition, or activity
*“Keep on working!”*
*“We continued to work into the night”*
3. **保存, 維持, 留下, 保持** — retain possession of
*“Can I keep my old stuffed animals?”*
*“She kept her maiden name after she married”*
4. **妨礙, 避免** — stop (someone or something) from doing something or being in a certain state
*“We must prevent the cancer from spreading”*
*“His snoring kept me from falling asleep”*
5. **奉行, 履行, 保持, 遵循, 遵守** — conform one's action or practice to
*“keep appointments”*
*“she never keeps her promises”*
6. **經營, 管理, 照顧, 保持** — look after; be the keeper of; have charge of
*“He keeps the shop when I am gone”*
*n.* 1. **生計** — the financial means whereby one lives
*“each child was expected to pay for their keep”*
*“he applied to the state for support”*
2. **城堡主塔, 城堡主樓, 城堡的主樓, 樓櫓** — the main tower within the walls of a medieval castle or fortress
3. **牢房** — a cell in a jail or prison

<a id="entry-k-000295"></a>
## keep an eye on

*v.* 1. **密切注視, 注意, 觀察** — follow with the eyes or the mind
*“Keep an eye on the baby, please!”*
*“The world is watching Sarajevo”*
2. *(idiomatic)* **關照 /關照** — To watch and pay attention to, in case of misbehaviour or mishap.

<a id="entry-k-000296"></a>
## keep away

*v.* 1. **防範, 不接近, 使離開** — prevent from coming close
*“I tried to keep the child away from the pool”*

<a id="entry-k-000297"></a>
## keep back

*v.* 1. **約束, 限制, 抑制** — keep under control; keep in check
*“suppress a smile”*
*“Keep your temper”*
2. **保留, 保持** — secure and keep for possible future use or application
*“The landlord retained the security deposit”*
*“I reserve the right to disagree”*
3. **扣下, 留下** — hold back; refuse to hand over or share
*“The father is withholding the allowance until the son cleans his room”*

## key

*n.* 1. **鑰匙** — metal device shaped in such a way that when it is inserted into the appropriate lock the lock's mechanism can be rotated
2. **音調, 音階** — any of 24 major or minor diatonic scales that provide the tonal framework for a piece of music
3. **一公斤** — a kilogram of a narcotic drug
*“they were carrying two keys of heroin”*
4. **略語表, 符號表** — a list of words or phrases that explain symbols or abbreviations
5. **拱心石, 拱頂石, 楔石** — the central building block at the top of an arch or vault
6. **鍵** — a lever (as in a keyboard) that actuates a mechanism when depressed
*adj.* 1. serving as an essential component
*“a cardinal rule”*
*“the central cause of the problem”*
*v.* 1. **提供鑰匙** — provide with a key
*“We were keyed after the locks were changed in the building”*
2. identify as in botany or biology, for example
3. vandalize a car by scratching the sides with a key
*“His new Mercedes was keyed last night in the parking lot”*
4. regulate the musical pitch of
5. harmonize with or adjust to
*“key one's actions to the voters' prevailing attitude”*

<a id="entry-k-000478"></a>

<a id="entry-k-000479"></a>

*adj.* 1. **定在某調+的, 調音調+的, 鍵控+的** — set to a key or tone
2. fitted with or secured by a key
*“a keyed instrument”*
*“the locks have not yet been keyed”*

<a id="entry-k-000481"></a>

*prop. n.* 1. **凱恩斯 /凱恩斯** — An English surname from Old French.

<a id="entry-k-000487"></a>
## Keynesian

**發音：** /ˈkeɪnziən/
*n.* 1. **凱恩斯主義者 /凱恩斯主義者** — A proponent of Keynesian economic doctrine.
*adj.* 1. **凱恩斯主義的 /凱恩斯主義的** — Of or pertaining to an economic theory based on the ideas of John Maynard Keynes, as put forward in his book The General Theory of Employment, Interest and Money, published in 1936 in response to the Great Depression of the 1930s, and extensively extended by a large body of followers before and after his death in 1946.

<a id="entry-k-000488"></a>
## keynesian

*n.* 1. a follower of the economic theories of John Maynard Keynes
*adj.* 1. **凱因斯+的, 凱恩斯主義+的** — of or relating to John Maynard Keynes or to his economic theories

<a id="entry-k-000489"></a>

**發音：** /ˈkeɪnziənɪz.m̩/
*n.* 1. **凱恩斯主義 /凱恩斯主義** — A prescriptive or normative economic stance according to which the state should actively stimulate economic growth and improve stability in the private sector through interest rates, taxation and public projects.

<a id="entry-k-000490"></a>
## keynote

**發音：** /ˈkiːˌnoʊt/
*v.* 1. set the keynote of
*“Comfort keynotes this designer's Fall collection”*
2. give the keynote address to (an audience)
*n.* 1. **主題演講 /主題演講** — A speech that sets the main theme of a conference or other gathering; a keynote speech or keynote address.
2. the principal theme in a speech or literary work
3. a fundamental or central idea
4. (music) the first note of a diatonic scale

## kick

**發音：** /kɪk/
*v.* 1. **踢** — thrash about or strike out with the feet
2. **踢, 踹, 蹴** — strike with the foot
*“The boy kicked the dog”*
*“Kick the door down”*
3. *(transitive)* **踢, 蹴** — To direct to a particular place by a blow with the foot or leg.
4. **踹, 踢** — kick a leg up
5. *(Internet, transitive)* **踢, 踢出, 移出** — To forcibly remove a participant from an online activity.
6. **發牢騷, 埋怨, 悲嘆, 抱怨** — express complaints, discontent, displeasure, or unhappiness
*“My mother complains all day”*
*“She has a lot to kick about”*
*n.* 1. **反衝, 後勁** — the sudden stimulation provided by strong drink (or certain drugs)
*“a sidecar is a smooth drink but it has a powerful kick”*
2. the act of delivering a blow with the foot
*“he gave the ball a powerful kick”*
*“the team's kicking was excellent”*
3. the swift release of a store of affective force
*“they got a great bang out of it”*
*“what a boot!”*
4. the backward jerk of a gun when it is fired
5. informal terms for objecting
*“I have a gripe about the service here”*
6. a rhythmic thrusting movement of the legs as in swimming or calisthenics
*“the kick must be synchronized with the arm movements”*
*“the swimmer's kicking left a wake behind him”*

<a id="entry-k-000585"></a>

*“Does the old man still kick around?”*

## kill

*v.* 1. **戕殺, 殺, 殺害, 殺死** — cause to die; put to death, usually intentionally or knowingly
*“This man killed several people when he tried to rob a bank”*
*“The farmer killed a pig for the holidays”*
2. **否決** — thwart the passage of
*“kill a motion”*
*“he shot down the student's proposal”*
3. **致命, 殺死** — be fatal
*“cigarettes kill”*
*“drunken driving kills”*
4. **使極不舒服, 使疼痛難忍, 折磨** — be the source of great pain for
*“These new shoes are killing me!”*
5. **徹底衰竭, 精疲力盡, 精疲力竭** — tire out completely
*“The daily stress of her work is killing her”*
6. **使終止, 使停止** — cause to cease operating
*“kill the engine”*
*n.* 1. the act of terminating a life
2. the destruction of an enemy plane or ship or tank or missile
*“the pilot reported two kills during the mission”*

<a id="entry-k-000662"></a>
## kilogram

**發音：** /ˈkɪləɡɹæm/
*n.* 1. **千克, 公斤** — In the International System of Units, the base unit of mass; conceived of as the mass of one litre of water, but now defined by taking the fixed numerical value of the Planck constant h to be 6.626 070 15 × 10⁻³⁴ when expressed in units of kg⋅m²⋅s⁻¹. Symbol: kg
2. **公斤, 千克** — one thousand grams; the basic unit of mass adopted under the Systeme International d'Unites
*“a kilogram is approximately 2.2 pounds”*

<a id="entry-k-000663"></a>

*“kinetic energy”*
2. *(not-comparable)* **運動的 /運動的, 動的 /動的** — Relating to motion.

<a id="entry-k-000712"></a>

<a id="entry-k-000713"></a>
## kinetics

2. the branch of mechanics concerned with the forces that cause motions of bodies

## kingdom

**發音：** /ˈkɪŋdəm/
*n.* 1. **王國, 領域** — a domain in which something is dominant
*“the untroubled kingdom of reason”*
*“a land of make-believe”*
2. **王國 /王國, 君主國 /君主國** — A realm having a king or queen as its actual or nominal sovereign.
3. **界** — A rank in the classification of organisms, below domain and above phylum; a taxon at that rank (e.g. the plant kingdom, the animal kingdom).
4. **君主國, 王國** — a monarchy with a king or queen as head of state
5. a country with a king as head of state
6. the domain ruled by a king or queen

4. **馬 /馬, 騎士 /騎士** — A chess piece, often in the shape of a horse's head, that is moved two squares in one direction and one at right angles to that direction in a single move, leaping over any intervening pieces.

## knock

**發音：** /nɒk/
*v.* 1. **敲, 用指關節敲打** — rap with the knuckles
*“knock on the door”*
2. *(intransitive)* **敲** — To rap one's knuckles against something, especially wood.
3. **輕打, 輕敲** — make light, repeated taps on a surface
*“he was tapping his fingers on the table impatiently”*
4. **發丁當聲, 發出爆聲, 咻地發聲** — sound like a car engine that is firing too early
*“the car pinged when I put in low-octane gasoline”*
*“The car pinked when the ignition was too far retarded”*
5. deliver a sharp blow or push :"He knocked the glass clear across the room"
6. knock against with force or violence
*“My car bumped into the tree”*
*n.* 1. the sound of knocking (as on a door or in an engine or bearing)
*“the knocking grew louder”*
2. negative criticism
3. a vigorous blow
*“the sudden knock floored him”*
*“he took a bash right in his face”*
4. a bad experience
*“the school of hard knocks”*
5. the act of hitting vigorously
*“he gave the table a whack”*

<a id="entry-k-000951"></a>
## knock about

*v.* 1. **健在, 活著** — be around; be alive or active
*“Does the old man still kick around?”*
2. strike against forcefully
*“Winds buffeted the tent”*

<a id="entry-k-000952"></a>
## knock back

*v.* 1. *(transitive)* **使目瞪口呆** — To stun; to surprise.
2. **使花費, 糜費, 耗費, 花費** — cost a certain amount
*“My daughter's wedding set me back $20,000”*
3. *(transitive)* **一飲而盡 /一飲而盡** — To drink an alcoholic beverage swiftly or often; to finish a beverage.
4. *(transitive)* **猛推後退 /猛推後退** — To push or strike someone or something backward with force.
5. *(transitive)* **克服** — To withstand or endure a difficult situation or setback.
6. *(transitive)* **大獲全勝 /大獲全勝** — To achieve a significant accomplishment or success.

<a id="entry-k-000953"></a>
## knock down

*v.* 1. *(informal, transitive)* **砍價** — To reduce the price of.
2. cause to come or go down
*“The policeman downed the heavily armed suspect”*
*“The mugger knocked down the old lady after she refused to hand over her wallet”*
3. knock down with force
*“He decked his opponent”*
4. shatter as if by explosion

<a id="entry-k-000954"></a>
## knock off

*v.* 1. **削減** — cut the price of
2. **偷竊, 行竊, 偷** — take by theft

*“drop a lawsuit”*

*“the double agent was neutralized”*
5. write quickly
*“She dashed off a note to her husband saying she would not be home for supper”*
*“He scratched off a thank-you note to the hostess”*

*“I know that I left the key on the table”*

3. **知道, 認識** — be familiar or acquainted with a person or an object

*“surprisingly knowledgeable about what was going on”*
5. thoroughly acquainted through study or experience
*“this girl, so intimate with nature"-W.H.Hudson”*
*“knowledgeable about the technique of painting"- Herbert Read”*
